Grounding(\n                    GroundingKind.SOURCE,\n                    \"sources/structured/permit_limits.csv\",\n                    f\"row {index} LIMIT_VALUE_ID={lvid}\",\n                )\n            ],\n        )\n\n    absent_measurements = 0\n    for index, row in enumerate(dmr_rows, start=2):\n        permit = row[\"EXTERNAL_PERMIT_NMBR\"]\n        feature_id = == ABSENT_REAL:\n            absent_measurements += 1\n        linked_limit = (\n            limit_value_id(row[\"LIMIT_VALUE_ID\"])\n            if row[\"LIMIT_VALUE_ID\"].strip()\n            else limit_value_id(\"0\")\n        )\n        # Ensure linked limit referent exists when LIMIT_VALUE_ID present\n        if row[\"LIMIT_VALUE_ID\"].strip():\n            tv.add_referent(\n      \"sources/structured/dmr_measurements.csv\",\n                        f\"row {index} LIMIT_VALUE_ID={row['LIMIT_VALUE_ID']}\",\n                    )\n                ],\n            )\n        tv.add_referent(\n            meas,\n            label=f\"DMR {row['DMR_EVENT_ID']}\",\n            grounding=[\n                Grounding(\n                    GroundingKind.SOURCE,\n                    \"sources/structured/dmr_measurements.csv\",\n                    f\"row {index} DMR_EVENT_ID={row['DMR_EVENT_ID']} DMR_VALUE_ID={dmr_value_id_val}\",\n                )\n            ],\n        )\n        tv.assert_tuple(\n            \"dmr_measurement_assertion\",\n            {\n                \"measurement\": meas,\n                \"facility\": facility_id(permit),\n               permit, row[\"MONITORING_PERIOD_END_DATE\"]\n                ),\n                \"limit_value\": linked_limit if row[\"LIMIT_VALUE_ID\"].strip() else meas,\n grounding=[\n                Grounding(\n                    GroundingKind.SOURCE,\n                    \"sources/structured/dmr_measurements.csv\",\n                    f\"row {index} notes.append(\n            f\"{absent_limit_values} permit_limit_assertion rows lacked LIMIT_VALUE_NMBR; \"\n            f\"stored sentinel {ABSENT_REAL} because TaskView REAL columns are NOT \"dmr_measurements\": len(dmr_rows),\n        \"notes\": notes,\n    }\n\n\ndef register_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"limit_period_overlaps_monitoring_period\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n            SELECT DISTINCT\n                pla.limit_value_id AS limit_value_id,\n                mpr.monitoring_period_id AS pla.limit_end_date\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"permit_limit_assertion\", \"monitoring_period_registry\"],\n    )\n\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"measurement_limit_structural_candidate\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n            SELECT DISTINCT\n                dma.measurement_id AS measurement_id,\n                pla.limit_value_id AS = pla.discharge_point_id\n             AND dma.parameter_id = pla.parameter_id\n            JOIN limit_period_overlaps_monitoring_period lpo\n              ON lpo.limit_value_id = pla.limit_value_id\n             AND lpo.monitoring_period_id = dma.monitoring_period_id\n            WHERE dma.limit_value_id = pla.limit_value_id\n               OR dma.limit_value_id = dma.measurement_id\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"dmr_measurement_assertion\",\n            \"permit_limit_assertion\",\n            \"limit_period_overlaps_monitoring_period\",\n        ],\n    )\n\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n            SELECT DISTINCT dma.measurement_id AS measurement_id\n            FROM dmr_measurement_assertion \"fy2025_monitoring_requirement\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n            SELECT DISTINCT lsr.monitoring_requirement_id AS monitoring_requirement_id\n            FROM limit_set_record lsr\n            JOIN pla.limit_end_date >= '{FY2025_START}'\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"limit_set_record\", \"permit_limit_assertion\"],\n    )\n\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"measurement_has_numeric_result\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n            SELECT DISTINCT measurement_id AS measurement_id\n            FROM dmr_measurement_assertion\n            WHERE \"monitoring_expectation_without_numeric_result\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n            SELECT DISTINCT\n                monitoring_expectation_id AS monitoring_expectation_id,\n                dma.facility_id AS facility_id,\n                dma.discharge_point_id AS discharge_point_id,\n                dma.parameter_id AS parameter_id,\n                dma.monitoring_period_id AS )\n\n\ndef run_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"limit_period_overlaps_monitoring_period\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"monitoring_period_registry\",\n            basis=\"Cartesian join on shared facility filtered by inclusive date overlap.\",\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"measurement_limit_structural_candidate\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n            basis=\"All structural candidates for in-scope measurements without selecting one.\",\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"dmr_measurement_assertion\",\n            basis=f\"Federal FY2025 \"measurement_has_numeric_result\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"dmr_measurement_assertion\",\n            basis=\"Non-sentinel DB_PATH.unlink()\n\n    notes: list[str] = []\n    with TaskView(DB_PATH, view_id=\"diligence-world\") as tv:\n        declare_from_vocabulary(tv, vocabulary)\n        base_stats = compile_base(tv, \"limit_period_overlaps_monitoring_period\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n            SELECT DISTINCT\n                pla.limit_value_id AS limit_value_id,\n                mpr.monitoring_period_id AS   tv.register_derivation(\n        \"limit_period_overlaps_monitoring_period\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n            SELECT DISTINCT\n                pla.limit_value_id AS limit_value_id,\n                mpr.monitoring_period_id AS monitoring_period_id,\n                mpr.period_end_date AS overlap_start,\n                mpr.period_end_date AS overlap_end\n            FROM permit_limit_assertion \"measurement_limit_structural_candidate\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n            SELECT DISTINCT\n                dma.measurement_id AS measurement_id,\n                pla.limit_value_id AS = pla.discharge_point_id\n             AND dma.parameter_id = pla.parameter_id\n            JOIN limit_period_overlaps_monitoring_period lpo\n              ON lpo.limit_value_id = pla.limit_value_id\n             AND lpo.monitoring_period_id = dma.monitoring_period_id\n            WHERE dma.limit_value_id = pla.limit_value_id\n               OR dma.limit_value_id = dma.measurement_id\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"dmr_measurement_assertion\",\n            \"permit_limit_assertion\",\n            \"limit_period_overlaps_monitoring_period\",\n        ],\n    )\n\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n            SELECT DISTINCT dma.measurement_id AS measurement_id\n            FROM dmr_measurement_assertion dma\n            JOIN monitoring_period_registry mpr\n              ON dma.monitoring_period_id = mpr.monitoring_period_id\n            WHERE mpr.period_end_date >= '{FY2025_START}'\n              AND mpr.period_end_date <= '{FY2025_END}'\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"dmr_measurement_assertion\", \"monitoring_period_registry\"],\n    )\n\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"fy2025_monitoring_requirement\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n            SELECT DISTINCT lsr.monitoring_requirement_id AS monitoring_requirement_id\n            FROM limit_set_record lsr\n            JOIN \"measurement_has_numeric_result\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n            SELECT DISTINCT measurement_id AS measurement_id\n            FROM dmr_measurement_assertion\n            WHERE \"monitoring_expectation_without_numeric_result\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n            SELECT DISTINCT\n                monitoring_expectation_id AS monitoring_expectation_id,\n                dma.facility_id AS facility_id,\n                dma.discharge_point_id AS discharge_point_id,\n                dma.parameter_id AS parameter_id,\n                dma.monitoring_period_id AS monitoring_period_id,\n                dma.measurement_id AS measurement_id\n            FROM dmr_measurement_assertion dma\n            JOIN fy2025_in_scope_measurement fy\n              ON )\n\n\ndef run_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"limit_period_overlaps_monitoring_period\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"monitoring_period_registry\",\n            basis=\"Cartesian join on shared facility filtered by inclusive date overlap.\",\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"measurement_limit_structural_candidate\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n            basis=\"All structural candidates for in-scope measurements without selecting one.\",\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"dmr_measurement_assertion\",\n            basis=f\"Federal FY2025 \"measurement_has_numeric_result\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"dmr_measurement_assertion\",\n            basis=\"Non-sentinel            CompletenessStatus.INCOMPLETE,\n            universe=\"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n            basis=\"FY2025 in-scope measurements lacking numeric results; schedule-implied slots not compiled.\",\n            known_gaps=(\"schedule_implied_monitoring_slots\",),\n        ),\n    )\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    vocabulary = load_vocabulary()\n    manifest = load_manifest()\n \"evidenced_operational_context not compiled in P2 (SEMANTIC construction_class).\"\n    )\n    notes.append(\n        \"Purpose relations (limit_applicability_judgment, )\n    notes.append(\n        \"Permit PDF prose not compiled; only structured CSV extracts and source_manifest facilities.\"\n    )\n\n    report = {\n        \"referents\": referent_count,\n )\n\n\ndef run_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"limit_period_overlaps_monitoring_period\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"monitoring_period_registry\",\n            basis=\"Cartesian join on shared facility filtered by inclusive date overlap.\",\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"measurement_limit_structural_candidate\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n            basis=\"All structural candidates for in-scope measurements without selecting one.\",\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"dmr_measurement_assertion\",\n            basis=f\"Federal FY2025 \"measurement_has_numeric_result\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"dmr_measurement_assertion\",\n            basis=\"Non-sentinel \"duplicate TaskView REFERENT physical columns.\"\n            )\n        mode = RelationMode.BASE if construction == \"MECHANICAL\" else RelationMode.DERIVED\n        tv.declare_relation(\n
